  7. CUP staff drug testing: no CUP-specific testing mandate located; the Flourish drug-free workplace summary is secondary and conflicts with the Justia §12.2 excerpt returned. [Verify] current §12.2.
  8. DSHS §300.303 lab ownership, §300.207 recall trigger/procedure, and §§300.301/300.302 adopted (vs proposed) wording: proposal text was returned; the adopted text must be checked in the March 2026 Texas Register adopted-rules page. Expired-COA / forged-result retest + detention/embargo under §431.048 is from proposal snippets; verify adopted wording. New: the April 2026 temporary restraining order vs the later THBC “remain enforceable” statement — check the SS Distro / THBC docket at publication for the current litigation status of §§300.101, 300.206(c), 300.301–300.303, 300.404 and 300.601(b).
  9. DSHS transfer / relocation / distributor (Chapter 431 wholesaler) question: no specific rule located. [Verify].
  10. TDA transfer / relocation, seed certification, indoor/greenhouse rule, transport manifest, and lab reporting deadline (14 days, secondary): [Verify] in 4 TAC Ch. 24 / TDA eApply.
  11. Pre-harvest timeline conflict: federal 30-day sampling window + 30-day harvest deadline (7 CFR §§990.3, 990.24, 990.26) vs Texas §24.23 harvest ≤15 days after sample collection, vs ACS “samples within 15 days” vs training material “samples within 30 days, minimum 8 plants, annual sampler renewal”. Resolve against current 4 TAC Ch. 24 before publishing a single timeline.
  12. DEA-registered lab conflict: 7 CFR Part 990 language (only DEA-registered labs after 31 December 2022, for USDA licensees and for state/Tribal plans) vs the given baseline (USDA enforcement delayed to 31 December 2026) vs Texas's TDA-registered lab system. [Verify] the Texas position for pre-harvest labs and for CHP labs separately.
  13. Hemp remediation: federal remediation (flower removal / shredding + retest) vs Texas §24.31 (“may not be further handled, processed, or enter stream of commerce, other than for disposal”). [Verify] whether remediation is available for a Texas lot. §24.31 itself is now directly opened (1 November 2026) and confirms the DEA reverse-distributor / 5-day / pay-costs / notify-USDA-and-TDA / records procedure.
  14. Hemp security code: no CUP-equivalent security rule located for DSHS/TDA hemp. A full-text search of 25 TAC Ch. 300 and 4 TAC Ch. 24 for “security” is a follow-up; state this as “no rule located”, not “no security required”.
  15. Hemp giveaways / discounts / promotions: no specific DSHS giveaway/discount rule located. Free-sample-to-adult treatment, loyalty/discount restrictions, and advertising content rules beyond labelling/21+/SB 2024 remain [Verify]. The SB 1316 tobacco outdoor-advertising claim (1,000 ft, schools/churches, effective 1 September 2025) is secondary and must not be presented as a hemp rule.
  16. Environmental MRLs / water / energy: no Texas hemp pesticide MRL table, hemp-specific water rule, or energy rule located. The EPA hemp label list (49 products in the January 2021 snapshot) must be re-checked against the current EPA hemp label list; the TDA limited-use list for hemp must be checked.
  17. Paraphernalia official-text check: §481.125 tiers are now resolved on Justia 2025 mirror text (Class C / Class A / state jail felony, repeat-delivery 90 days–1 year), but the official statutes.capitol.texas.gov check remains. Transportation Code §521.372 180-day suspension claim remains [Verify]. The TCUP inhalation-device carve-out remains [Verify] (reasoning from “in violation of this chapter”, not a quoted carve-out).
  18. DWI “any detectable THC” vs “presence ≠ impairment”: secondary summaries conflict on the §49.01 interpretation. Check the official §49.01 definition and recent Texas appellate drug-DWI case law before stating either reading as law. Implied-consent / refusal licence consequences (Transportation Code Ch. 724) and the full §49.04 penalty tiers (including enhancements) were NOT returned; [Verify].
  19. Expunction holding: Ex parte R.P.G.P., No. 19-1051, majority holding NOT established from the dissent PDF and search summaries alone; Ex parte M.A.S. (2021) summary emphasises an arrest-based approach that sits uneasily with R.P.G.P.'s “neither entirely arrest-based nor offense-based” discussion. Open both opinions and the current Chapter 55A text before republication. The Chapter 55A replacement date (1 January 2025, per secondary sources) and the §411.072 / §411.0725 / §411.0726 / §411.073 / §411.074 waiting periods, “one order” limit, and clerk/DPS transmission timelines (15 / 10 / 30 business days, secondary) all remain [Verify] in official text. The Land & Law “small amounts qualify after probation” bullet must NOT be repeated.
  20. Cite-and-release: HB 2391 year corrected to 2007 (80th Legislature, effective 1 September 2007), NOT 2017. The Senate vote (29–1) and signing date (15 June 2007, Governor Rick Perry) are from a petition summary, [Verify] in the Senate journal / signing record. The full Article 14.06(d) offence list was truncated in the return; [Verify]. The current status of the San Marcos mandatory cite-and-release ordinance after the 2025 decriminalisation litigation remains [Verify]. No statewide list of cite-and-release departments was located (Unavailable, not “none”).
  21. Landlord/tenant: no Texas tenant TCUP protection or landlord accommodation duty located; no controlling appellate holding on non-smoked TCUP eviction located. The federally assisted housing “Section 577” citation is truncated/unverified; check the Quality Housing and Work Responsibility Act / HUD text before citing a section number. Ordinary eviction procedure is Property Code Ch. 24 generally; no cannabis-specific eviction timeline located.
  22. Guns: the ATF interim “unlawful user” rule effective 22 January 2026 (regular, ongoing use) is secondary only; [Verify] in the Federal Register. The DPS LTC position is now confirmed as a DPS FAQ position via secondary quotation (participation alone does not disqualify; underlying-condition / Medical Advisory Board caveat applies) — still [Verify] against the live DPS LTC FAQ page itself. The Schedule III effect on §922(g)(3) / Form 4473 was NOT located; [Verify]. United States v. Hemani is PENDING as of the March 2026 article (arguments 2 March 2026, decision expected late June); check whether a decision has issued by publication date, and do NOT present predictions as holdings. The search-return claim about a March 2026 executive order directing rescheduling must NOT be imported; use the given federal baseline (Schedule III medical effective 28 April 2026, 91 FR 22714).
  23. Pets: no Texas TBVME CBD guidance, no Texas pet-food hemp adoption, and no Texas pet-CBD testing/labelling/age rule located. The TBVME compounded-controlled-substances clarification (rule 573.44, April meeting) is NOT CBD guidance.   24. Schools: §487.201 / TASB FFAC (Legal) “may not ban authorised low-THC possession” is fixed on TASB policy pages returned, now confirmed again in Connally ISD (issued 22 May 2025) and Mesquite ISD (issued 11 June 2024) FFAC (Legal) pages, but whether §487.201 has been applied to school districts by a court/AG opinion remains [Verify]. No statewide school administration/storage/self-administration mandate for TCUP was located; Education Code Ch. 38, DPS rules, TEA guidance, and district FFAC (Local) policies must be checked. CBD-ban guidance is TASB “may likely” guidance, not a statute. Section 504 / IEP treatment of TCUP, drug-free-zone application to authorised TCUP possession, and individual university campus policies were NOT located.
  25. University / state-funded medical research: no Texas state-funded TCUP medical research programme was located. CHAIN is USDA NIFA-funded (federal), not state-funded. Ibogaine record corrected: HB 3717 is DEAD (laid on the table 12 May 2025); SB 2308 was signed June 2025 ($50 million, matched); December 2025 UTHealth Houston / UTMB award; 31 March 2026 announcement of intent to fund $100 million total — all psychedelic, NOT cannabis.
